### Recipe and assay scope

evidence: concern. Targets: CultureMech:015430.

Formulation_unresolved: Two 24-ingredient Tris-buffered artificial seawater owners exchange sulfolactate and acetate; abstract does not enumerate base salts, vitamins, final amounts or the acetate comparator. Purified SlcC/ComC/SuyAB enzyme substrates and activities are not cultivation ingredients or doses. No exact local recipe or acetate-assay assignment established.

HMP contamination in thiamin stock is explicit; nominal thiamin growth is not evidence of HMP independence. Selected cultivation, abstract and contamination passages read; no exhaustive whole-paper organism inventory or linked-figure/supplement inspection. Base Carini 2013 HTML/Markdown retained; its glycine-substitution experiments are distinct from this 2014 HMP study. Existing 2013 glycine/serine and 2014 alternate-phosphorus variants are not automatically matching destinations.

## Findings

### formulation-5: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 5

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-5.

Formulation_unresolved: Two 24-ingredient Tris-buffered artificial seawater owners exchange sulfolactate and acetate; abstract does not enumerate base salts, vitamins, final amounts or the acetate comparator. Sulfolactate stereochemistry and the imported chemical identity require reconciliation. Abstract only. Purified SlcC/ComC/SuyAB enzyme substrates and activities are not cultivation ingredients or doses. No exact local recipe or acetate-assay assignment established. 

### formulation-83: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 83

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-83.

Formulation_unresolved: The inorganic/Fex5 owners already have reciprocal parent/child CONCENTRATION_VARIANT links and differ in ferrous-sulfate amount. Preserve those links. Abstract nitrate/H2 anaerobic experiments cannot be assigned to local 13-ingredient recipes that do not specify nitrate or gas composition; no gas amount or missing nutrient is invented. Abstract does not enumerate the two recipes or certify the iron difference. Existing variant relationship is retained, not newly scientifically endorsed. Publisher HTML is an abstract/subscription preview, not full methods. Existing reciprocal inorganic/Fex5 links are unchanged, not newly certified. 

### formulation-139: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 139

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-139.

Formulation_unresolved: The two 39-ingredient freshwater owners combine multiple alternative electron donors and acceptors. Methods and the independent-experiment statement do not establish that all were added together. Cobalt sulfate versus cobalt chloride and all inherited base quantities remain unresolved. No composite-assay recipe is certified. Selected full-XML methods and model sections inspected, not figures/supplements. Base reference 51 is Lovley 1993 PMID:8387263, doi:10.1007/BF00290916; verified archived abstract does not enumerate the freshwater formula. HPLC eluents, isotonic wash, SDS lysis and model flux constraints must remain separate from medium. The returned strain node also lists DSM 15236; matching is justified by ATCC BAA-621. 

### formulation-140: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 140

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-140.

Formulation_unresolved: The 12-component MR and 13-component nitrogen-limited MR owners require reconciliation of ammonium-phosphate identity, magnesium-sulfate hydration, citrate acid/anion, sodium-phosphate mass basis and inherited trace stock. The explicit nitrogen replacement defines a study difference, but exact local-parent assignment remains unresolved; do not correct molar amounts from an unverified molecular form. Selected full-XML methods and results inspected, not linked supplements/images. Base reference 13 is Lee and Lee 1996 doi:10.1007/BF02074874; its newly archived publisher abstract/preview does not enumerate trace stock. KOH titration, antifoam and analytical GC/HPLC reagents cannot be conflated with final base ingredients. KCTC 22469 is the reported culture accession, not an independently verified NCBI synonym. 

### formulation-150: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 150

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-150.

Formulation_unresolved: Both 20-ingredient AMS1 pyruvate owners contain nominal thiamin, at different imported amounts, and differ in glycine versus serine. Retrieved 2014 cultivation methods use oxaloacetate, not these nominal pyruvate formulations, without a general vitamin mixture and with experiment-specific thiamin/precursor additions. HMP contamination in thiamin stock is explicit; nominal thiamin growth is not evidence of HMP independence. Reuse of previously archived full PMC HTML supersedes abstract-only access caused by this turn's XML-route failure. Selected cultivation, abstract and contamination passages read; no exhaustive whole-paper organism inventory or linked-figure/supplement inspection. Base Carini 2013 HTML/Markdown retained; its glycine-substitution experiments are distinct from this 2014 HMP study. Existing 2013 glycine/serine and 2014 alternate-phosphorus variants are not automatically matching destinations. 

### formulation-3: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 3

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-3.

Formulation_unresolved: FWAFC, ammonium-free FWAFC and ammonium-plus-gaseous-nitrogen-free FWAFC are distinct experiments. NBAFYE propagation/plating and competence/recovery media are separate. The local ferric-dicitrate identity/amount and inherited vitamin/trace formulation remain unresolved. Do not normalize the source's unusual displayed phosphate/iron formulas without checking original scientific context. A figure caption's antibiotic mass-unit wording conflicts with the methods wording; do not inherit either blindly. Selected full-HTML strain table, culturing methods and results inspected. Base references 17 and 19 remain reference-list leads, not inherited formula evidence. Collection identity: https://www.atcc.org/products/51573, accessed_on=2026-10-13; archived at references_cache/papers/taxonomy/coppi-atcc-51573-20261013.html and .md. Negative nitrogen-fixation phenotypes are not positive growth on nitrogen-containing parent medium. 

### formulation-8: Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 8

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-8.

Formulation_unresolved: Primary growth experiment cites a minimal succinate broth base, reference 7. A separate null-mutant survival experiment uses commercial Davis minimal broth with succinate. Do not combine those media or inherit all 15 local amounts from one shared carbon-source value. Reporter substrate measurements and cell survival are distinct from recipe concentration and a universal growth rate. The recovered HTML contains the short article body despite the archive helper's initial preview heuristic. Base reference 7 remains a reference-list lead; full formula and supplements have not been verified. Six-week population dynamics and reporter measurements are not a universal growth rate or requirement.

### leitch-strain-conflict: Combined strain/accession designation is unresolved

major / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-leitch-strain-conflict.

The paper title pairs Bacillus subtilis (168) with NCIMB 12900. NBIMCC's historical catalog links NCIMB 12900 to ATCC 19659/PRD66; current ATCC identifies that accession as Bacillus spizizenii, whereas ATCC 23857 is Bacillus subtilis strain 168. This is an unresolved accession/species conflict, not proof which organism the authors actually used. Do not force either exact strain node or treat reported-name species 1423 as a settled modern parent for the combined designation. Preserve the source spelling, separate reported-name mapping from physical-strain identity, and use verified Bacillus genus 1386 as the conservative parent-only fallback if needed. Verify the original full paper and NCIMB accession history before resolving.

### htcc7211-role-inventory: Extend HTCC7211 role notes with the separate exudation assay

minor / open / confirmed; issue key: environmental-study-citations-htcc7211-role-inventory.

# Additional Assay Context

Source: PMID:24781899, doi:10.1038/ismej.2014.61.

Archived full source: &#96;references_cache/papers/primary_html/carini-2014-pmc.html&#96;;
Markdown: &#96;references_cache/papers/primary_html/carini-2014-pmc.md&#96;.

Selected results text and Table 2 were additionally inspected after the citation
preview. HTCC7211 is not only a comparative-genome subject: the article also
reports a separate culture-supernatant HMP-exudation test, with HMP undetected
after growth. This is not a negative-growth result. The native note identifies
its comparative role and correctly withholds a growth edge to these local
pyruvate recipes, but does not yet inventory this additional assay role.

The verified NCBI node remains 439493, Candidatus Pelagibacter sp. HTCC7211,
actual species rank. This observation neither resolves the cultivation medium
for that assay nor establishes that it matches either thiamin/pyruvate owner.
Reconcile the assay's medium and supplement scope before extending variant or
growth evidence. No new citation, native growth assertion or concentration
certification is claimed for this follow-up.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Partial self-review, not independent adversarial review or full recipe approval.
- Selected full-text passages and four abstract-only sources; linked images/supplements and complete base formulas not certified.
- Qualified parent mappings and source-surface rank discrepancies retained. Bounded searches do not prove taxon absence.
- Existing inorganic/Fex5 links preserved without new scientific endorsement. No source or generated page publication.
- Candidate queues overlap, include ancillary identifiers and require primary review; neither count proves citation exhaustion.

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: Two 24-ingredient Tris-buffered artificial seawater
    owners exchange sulfolactate and acetate; abstract does not enumerate base salts,
    vitamins, final amounts or the acetate comparator. Sulfolactate stereochemistry
    and the imported chemical identity require reconciliation. Abstract only. Purified
    SlcC/ComC/SuyAB enzyme substrates and activities are not cultivation ingredients
    or doses. No exact local recipe or acetate-assay assignment established. '

    owners exchange sulfolactate and acetate; abstract does not enumerate base salts,
    vitamins, final amounts or the acetate comparator. Sulfolactate stereochemistry
    and the imported chemical identity require reconciliation. Abstract only. Purified
    SlcC/ComC/SuyAB enzyme substrates and activities are not cultivation ingredients
    or doses. No exact local recipe or acetate-assay assignment established. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: The inorganic/Fex5 owners already have reciprocal
    parent/child CONCENTRATION_VARIANT links and differ in ferrous-sulfate amount.
    Preserve those links. Abstract nitrate/H2 anaerobic experiments cannot be assigned
    to local 13-ingredient recipes that do not specify nitrate or gas composition;
    no gas amount or missing nutrient is invented. Abstract does not enumerate the
    two recipes or certify the iron difference. Existing variant relationship is retained,
    not newly scientifically endorsed. Publisher HTML is an abstract/subscription
    preview, not full methods. Existing reciprocal inorganic/Fex5 links are unchanged,
    not newly certified. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: The two 39-ingredient freshwater owners combine
    multiple alternative electron donors and acceptors. Methods and the independent-experiment
    statement do not establish that all were added together. Cobalt sulfate versus
    cobalt chloride and all inherited base quantities remain unresolved. No composite-assay
    recipe is certified. Selected full-XML methods and model sections inspected, not
    figures/supplements. Base reference 51 is Lovley 1993 PMID:8387263, doi:10.1007/BF00290916;
    verified archived abstract does not enumerate the freshwater formula. HPLC eluents,
    isotonic wash, SDS lysis and model flux constraints must remain separate from
    medium. The returned strain node also lists DSM 15236; matching is justified by
    ATCC BAA-621. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: The two 39-ingredient freshwater owners combine
    multiple alternative electron donors and acceptors. Methods and the independent-experiment
    statement do not establish that all were added together. Cobalt sulfate versus
    cobalt chloride and all inherited base quantities remain unresolved. No composite-assay
    recipe is certified. Selected full-XML methods and model sections inspected, not
    figures/supplements. Base reference 51 is Lovley 1993 PMID:8387263, doi:10.1007/BF00290916;
    verified archived abstract does not enumerate the freshwater formula. HPLC eluents,
    isotonic wash, SDS lysis and model flux constraints must remain separate from
    medium. The returned strain node also lists DSM 15236; matching is justified by
    ATCC BAA-621. '

  - claims
  - excerpts
  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: The 12-component MR and 13-component nitrogen-limited
    MR owners require reconciliation of ammonium-phosphate identity, magnesium-sulfate
    hydration, citrate acid/anion, sodium-phosphate mass basis and inherited trace
    stock. The explicit nitrogen replacement defines a study difference, but exact
    local-parent assignment remains unresolved; do not correct molar amounts from
    an unverified molecular form. Selected full-XML methods and results inspected,
    not linked supplements/images. Base reference 13 is Lee and Lee 1996 doi:10.1007/BF02074874;
    its newly archived publisher abstract/preview does not enumerate trace stock.
    KOH titration, antifoam and analytical GC/HPLC reagents cannot be conflated with
    final base ingredients. KCTC 22469 is the reported culture accession, not an independently
    verified NCBI synonym. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: The 12-component MR and 13-component nitrogen-limited
    MR owners require reconciliation of ammonium-phosphate identity, magnesium-sulfate
    hydration, citrate acid/anion, sodium-phosphate mass basis and inherited trace
    stock. The explicit nitrogen replacement defines a study difference, but exact
    local-parent assignment remains unresolved; do not correct molar amounts from
    an unverified molecular form. Selected full-XML methods and results inspected,
    not linked supplements/images. Base reference 13 is Lee and Lee 1996 doi:10.1007/BF02074874;
    its newly archived publisher abstract/preview does not enumerate trace stock.
    KOH titration, antifoam and analytical GC/HPLC reagents cannot be conflated with
    final base ingredients. KCTC 22469 is the reported culture accession, not an independently
    verified NCBI synonym. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: Both 20-ingredient AMS1 pyruvate owners contain
    nominal thiamin, at different imported amounts, and differ in glycine versus serine.
    Retrieved 2014 cultivation methods use oxaloacetate, not these nominal pyruvate
    formulations, without a general vitamin mixture and with experiment-specific thiamin/precursor
    additions. HMP contamination in thiamin stock is explicit; nominal thiamin growth
    is not evidence of HMP independence. Reuse of previously archived full PMC HTML
    supersedes abstract-only access caused by this turn''s XML-route failure. Selected
    cultivation, abstract and contamination passages read; no exhaustive whole-paper
    organism inventory or linked-figure/supplement inspection. Base Carini 2013 HTML/Markdown
    retained; its glycine-substitution experiments are distinct from this 2014 HMP
    study. Existing 2013 glycine/serine and 2014 alternate-phosphorus variants are
    not automatically matching destinations. '

  summary: 'Candidatus Pelagibacter ubique HTCC1062: Candidatus Pelagibacter ubique
    HTCC1062, NCBITaxon:335992, actual rank strain; exact_strain; HMP study organism;
    not a certified local pyruvate recipe. HTCC7211: Candidatus Pelagibacter sp. HTCC7211,
    NCBITaxon:439493, actual rank species; verified_name_at_actual_species_rank; comparative-genome
    mention, not local-medium growth. Synechococcus sp. WH8102: Parasynechococcus
    marenigrum WH 8102, NCBITaxon:84588, actual rank strain; verified_equivalent_name;
    separate exudation assay medium. Prochlorococcus sp. MED4: Prochlorococcus marinus
    subsp. pastoris str. CCMP1986, NCBITaxon:59919, actual rank strain; verified_MED4_synonym;
    separate exudation assay; reported CCMP2389 accession independently unresolved.
    Dunaliella tertiolecta CCMP1320: Dunaliella tertiolecta, NCBITaxon:3047, actual
    rank species; parent_rank_only; species parent only; exact strain unresolved;
    separate exudation medium. OM43 HTCC2181: Methylophilales bacterium HTCC2181,
    NCBITaxon:383631, actual rank species; verified_name_at_actual_species_rank; separate
    exudation assay medium. Escherichia coli: Escherichia coli, NCBITaxon:562, actual
    rank species; species_name; sequence-query comparison, not local growth. Bacillus
    subtilis: Bacillus subtilis, NCBITaxon:1423, actual rank species; species_name;
    sequence-query comparison, not local growth. Saccharomyces cerevisiae: Saccharomyces
    cerevisiae, NCBITaxon:4932, actual rank species; species_name; sequence-query
    comparison, not local growth. Additional HTCC7211 exudation role documented in
    the follow-up; native notes currently cover its comparative role.'

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: FWAFC, ammonium-free FWAFC and ammonium-plus-gaseous-nitrogen-free
    FWAFC are distinct experiments. NBAFYE propagation/plating and competence/recovery
    media are separate. The local ferric-dicitrate identity/amount and inherited vitamin/trace
    formulation remain unresolved. Do not normalize the source''s unusual displayed
    phosphate/iron formulas without checking original scientific context. A figure
    caption''s antibiotic mass-unit wording conflicts with the methods wording; do
    not inherit either blindly. Selected full-HTML strain table, culturing methods
    and results inspected. Base references 17 and 19 remain reference-list leads,
    not inherited formula evidence. Collection identity: https://www.atcc.org/products/51573,
    accessed_on=2026-10-13; archived at references_cache/papers/taxonomy/coppi-atcc-51573-20261013.html
    and .md. Negative nitrogen-fixation phenotypes are not positive growth on nitrogen-containing
    parent medium. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: Primary growth experiment cites a minimal succinate
    broth base, reference 7. A separate null-mutant survival experiment uses commercial
    Davis minimal broth with succinate. Do not combine those media or inherit all
    15 local amounts from one shared carbon-source value. Reporter substrate measurements
    and cell survival are distinct from recipe concentration and a universal growth
    rate. The recovered HTML contains the short article body despite the archive helper''s
    initial preview heuristic. Base reference 7 remains a reference-list lead; full
    formula and supplements have not been verified. Six-week population dynamics and
    reporter measurements are not a universal growth rate or requirement. '

  summary: 'Formulation_unresolved: The abstract describes a defined medium with one
    added amino acid and compares Fang and Demain medium; it does not enumerate the
    two local formulas or establish the Dawes and Mandelstam owner as that comparator.
    Local glucose, tryptophan, magnesium chloride, phosphate and citrate amounts differ;
    units, chemical forms and exact study-to-owner assignment remain unresolved. No
    large-factor correction is justified by the abstract. Historical source rows label
    a doubling time with reciprocal-hour units; no metric is imported. The title''s
    combined designation conflicts with collection evidence: NBIMCC catalog 3562 equates
    NCIMB 12900 with ATCC 19659/PRD66, currently called Bacillus spizizenii by ATCC;
    ATCC 23857 is B. subtilis strain 168. This does not prove which organism the authors
    used; neither exact strain nor an unqualified B. subtilis species assignment is
    made. Collection sources=https://www.atcc.org/products/19659; https://www.atcc.org/products/23857;
    https://www.nbimcc.org/cat/Catalog%272005/Bacteria/Bacillus%20subtilis.html. Raw
    HTML and selected identity Markdown: references_cache/papers/taxonomy/leitch-atcc-19659-20261013.html/.md,
    leitch-atcc-23857-20261013.html/.md, leitch-nbimcc-3562-20261013.html/.md. Full
    paper and NCIMB accession history are needed to resolve the conflict. Publisher/Crossref
    metadata identifies the paper; PMID lookup remains unresolved, not proven absent. '

    added amino acid and compares Fang and Demain medium; it does not enumerate the
    two local formulas or establish the Dawes and Mandelstam owner as that comparator.
    Local glucose, tryptophan, magnesium chloride, phosphate and citrate amounts differ;
    units, chemical forms and exact study-to-owner assignment remain unresolved. No
    large-factor correction is justified by the abstract. Historical source rows label
    a doubling time with reciprocal-hour units; no metric is imported. The title''s
    combined designation conflicts with collection evidence: NBIMCC catalog 3562 equates
    NCIMB 12900 with ATCC 19659/PRD66, currently called Bacillus spizizenii by ATCC;
    ATCC 23857 is B. subtilis strain 168. This does not prove which organism the authors
    used; neither exact strain nor an unqualified B. subtilis species assignment is
    made. Collection sources=https://www.atcc.org/products/19659; https://www.atcc.org/products/23857;

  - CultureMech:015431
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: Two 24-ingredient Tris-buffered artificial
    seawater owners exchange sulfolactate and acetate; abstract does not enumerate
    base salts, vitamins, final amounts or the acetate comparator. Sulfolactate stereochemistry
    and the imported chemical identity require reconciliation. Abstract only. Purified
    SlcC/ComC/SuyAB enzyme substrates and activities are not cultivation ingredients
    or doses. No exact local recipe or acetate-assay assignment established. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-5
  - claims
  owner_paths:

    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/specialized/tris_buffered_artificial_seawater_with_nh4cl_and_acetate.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: formulation-83
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-83
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 83'
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007121
  - CultureMech:007122
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: The inorganic/Fex5 owners already have reciprocal
    parent/child CONCENTRATION_VARIANT links and differ in ferrous-sulfate amount.
    Preserve those links. Abstract nitrate/H2 anaerobic experiments cannot be assigned
    to local 13-ingredient recipes that do not specify nitrate or gas composition;
    no gas amount or missing nutrient is invented. Abstract does not enumerate the
    two recipes or certify the iron difference. Existing variant relationship is retained,
    not newly scientifically endorsed. Publisher HTML is an abstract/subscription
    preview, not full methods. Existing reciprocal inorganic/Fex5 links are unchanged,
    not newly certified. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-83
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/inorganic_medium.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/fex5_medium.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: formulation-139
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-139
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 139'
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007293
  - CultureMech:007294
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: The two 39-ingredient freshwater owners combine
    multiple alternative electron donors and acceptors. Methods and the independent-experiment
    statement do not establish that all were added together. Cobalt sulfate versus
    cobalt chloride and all inherited base quantities remain unresolved. No composite-assay
    recipe is certified. Selected full-XML methods and model sections inspected, not
    figures/supplements. Base reference 51 is Lovley 1993 PMID:8387263, doi:10.1007/BF00290916;
    verified archived abstract does not enumerate the freshwater formula. HPLC eluents,
    isotonic wash, SDS lysis and model flux constraints must remain separate from
    medium. The returned strain node also lists DSM 15236; matching is justified by
    ATCC BAA-621. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-139
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/defined_freshwater_medium_coso4.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/defined_freshwater_medium_cocl2.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: formulation-140
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-140
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 140'
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007220
  - CultureMech:007295
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: The 12-component MR and 13-component nitrogen-limited
    MR owners require reconciliation of ammonium-phosphate identity, magnesium-sulfate
    hydration, citrate acid/anion, sodium-phosphate mass basis and inherited trace
    stock. The explicit nitrogen replacement defines a study difference, but exact
    local-parent assignment remains unresolved; do not correct molar amounts from
    an unverified molecular form. Selected full-XML methods and results inspected,
    not linked supplements/images. Base reference 13 is Lee and Lee 1996 doi:10.1007/BF02074874;
    its newly archived publisher abstract/preview does not enumerate trace stock.
    KOH titration, antifoam and analytical GC/HPLC reagents cannot be conflated with
    final base ingredients. KCTC 22469 is the reported culture accession, not an independently
    verified NCBI synonym. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-140
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/mr_medium_d_fructose.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/nitrogen_limited_mr_medium.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: formulation-150
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-150
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 150'
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007383
  - CultureMech:007385
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: Both 20-ingredient AMS1 pyruvate owners contain
    nominal thiamin, at different imported amounts, and differ in glycine versus serine.
    Retrieved 2014 cultivation methods use oxaloacetate, not these nominal pyruvate
    formulations, without a general vitamin mixture and with experiment-specific thiamin/precursor
    additions. HMP contamination in thiamin stock is explicit; nominal thiamin growth
    is not evidence of HMP independence. Reuse of previously archived full PMC HTML
    supersedes abstract-only access caused by this turn''s XML-route failure. Selected
    cultivation, abstract and contamination passages read; no exhaustive whole-paper
    organism inventory or linked-figure/supplement inspection. Base Carini 2013 HTML/Markdown
    retained; its glycine-substitution experiments are distinct from this 2014 HMP
    study. Existing 2013 glycine/serine and 2014 alternate-phosphorus variants are
    not automatically matching destinations. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-150
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/MEDIADB_476_AMS1_Pyruvate_Glycine.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/MEDIADB_478_AMS1_Pyruvate_Serine.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: formulation-3
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-3
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 3'
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007301
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: FWAFC, ammonium-free FWAFC and ammonium-plus-gaseous-nitrogen-free
    FWAFC are distinct experiments. NBAFYE propagation/plating and competence/recovery
    media are separate. The local ferric-dicitrate identity/amount and inherited vitamin/trace
    formulation remain unresolved. Do not normalize the source''s unusual displayed
    phosphate/iron formulas without checking original scientific context. A figure
    caption''s antibiotic mass-unit wording conflicts with the methods wording; do
    not inherit either blindly. Selected full-HTML strain table, culturing methods
    and results inspected. Base references 17 and 19 remain reference-list leads,
    not inherited formula evidence. Collection identity: https://www.atcc.org/products/51573,
    accessed_on=2026-10-13; archived at references_cache/papers/taxonomy/coppi-atcc-51573-20261013.html
    and .md. Negative nitrogen-fixation phenotypes are not positive growth on nitrogen-containing
    parent medium. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-3
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/fwafc.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: formulation-8
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-formulation-8
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Exact study-to-local-recipe assignment remains unresolved: source 8'
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007229
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: Primary growth experiment cites a minimal
    succinate broth base, reference 7. A separate null-mutant survival experiment
    uses commercial Davis minimal broth with succinate. Do not combine those media
    or inherit all 15 local amounts from one shared carbon-source value. Reporter
    substrate measurements and cell survival are distinct from recipe concentration
    and a universal growth rate. The recovered HTML contains the short article body
    despite the archive helper''s initial preview heuristic. Base reference 7 remains
    a reference-list lead; full formula and supplements have not been verified. Six-week
    population dynamics and reporter measurements are not a universal growth rate
    or requirement. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-8
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/minimal_medium_lostroh_et_al.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe

  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007192
  field_paths:
  - ingredients
  - references
  description: 'Formulation_unresolved: Publisher abstract does not enumerate the
    seven local amounts. Glucose-dependent ethanol kinetics in minimal versus complete
    medium do not certify the imported mM values, base preparation or an exact local
    variant. NCIMB 11163 node 622759 is a candidate, not an assigned identity. For
    ATCC 10988 and CP4, archived current EFetch XML returns strain rank; an earlier
    browser surface displayed no rank. The source-surface discrepancy is retained,
    not hidden. Publisher/Crossref author spelling Nilton A. Typas and literal title
    inZymomonas are retained without an unverified correction. PMID lookup remains
    unresolved, not proven absent. '
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-103
  - claims
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/galani_mm.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: leitch-strain-conflict
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-leitch-strain-conflict
  category: evidence
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Combined strain/accession designation is unresolved
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007067
  - CultureMech:007068
  field_paths:
  - references
  description: The paper title pairs Bacillus subtilis (168) with NCIMB 12900. NBIMCC's
    historical catalog links NCIMB 12900 to ATCC 19659/PRD66; current ATCC identifies
    that accession as Bacillus spizizenii, whereas ATCC 23857 is Bacillus subtilis
    strain 168. This is an unresolved accession/species conflict, not proof which
    organism the authors actually used. Do not force either exact strain node or treat
    reported-name species 1423 as a settled modern parent for the combined designation.
    Preserve the source spelling, separate reported-name mapping from physical-strain
    identity, and use verified Bacillus genus 1386 as the conservative parent-only
    fallback if needed. Verify the original full paper and NCIMB accession history
    before resolving.
  evidence_ids:
  - paper-67
  - leitch-collection
  - taxonomy-3
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/leitch_et_al.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/dawes_and_mandelstam_medium_from_leitch_et_al.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
- finding_id: htcc7211-role-inventory
  issue_key: environmental-study-citations-htcc7211-role-inventory
  category: evidence
  severity: minor
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Extend HTCC7211 role notes with the separate exudation assay
  target_ids:
  - CultureMech:007383
  - CultureMech:007385
  field_paths:
  - references
  description: '# Additional Assay Context


    Source: PMID:24781899, doi:10.1038/ismej.2014.61.


    Archived full source: `references_cache/papers/primary_html/carini-2014-pmc.html`;

    Markdown: `references_cache/papers/primary_html/carini-2014-pmc.md`.


    Selected results text and Table 2 were additionally inspected after the citation

    preview. HTCC7211 is not only a comparative-genome subject: the article also

    reports a separate culture-supernatant HMP-exudation test, with HMP undetected

    after growth. This is not a negative-growth result. The native note identifies

    its comparative role and correctly withholds a growth edge to these local

    pyruvate recipes, but does not yet inventory this additional assay role.


    The verified NCBI node remains 439493, Candidatus Pelagibacter sp. HTCC7211,

    actual species rank. This observation neither resolves the cultivation medium

    for that assay nor establishes that it matches either thiamin/pyruvate owner.

    Reconcile the assay''s medium and supplement scope before extending variant or

    growth evidence. No new citation, native growth assertion or concentration

    certification is claimed for this follow-up.

    '
  evidence_ids:
  - body-150
  - additional-assay
  owner_paths:
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/MEDIADB_476_AMS1_Pyruvate_Glycine.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe
  - repository: culturebotai/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/MEDIADB_478_AMS1_Pyruvate_Serine.yaml
    role: authoritative normalized recipe

limitations:
- Partial self-review, not independent adversarial review or full recipe approval.
- Selected full-text passages and four abstract-only sources; linked images/supplements
  and complete base formulas not certified.
- Qualified parent mappings and source-surface rank discrepancies retained. Bounded
  searches do not prove taxon absence.
- Existing inorganic/Fex5 links preserved without new scientific endorsement. No source
  or generated page publication.
- Candidate queues overlap, include ancillary identifiers and require primary review;
  neither count proves citation exhaustion.
